<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-naumi-shri-gurupadabjam">науми ш́рӣ-гурупа̄да̄бджам̇, йатира̄джеш́вареш́варам<span class="page-num"> — 275</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-nitai-gunamani-amara-nitai-gunamani">нита̄и гун̣аман̣и а̄ма̄ра нита̄и гун̣аман̣и<span class="page-num"> — 113</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-nitai-pada-kamala-koti-chandra-sushitala">нита̄и-пада-камала, кот̣ӣ-чандра-суш́ӣтала<span class="page-num"> — 111</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-nite-yasmin-nishiante-nayana-jala-bharaih">нӣте йасмин ниш́а̄нте найана-джала-бхараих̣ сна̄та-га̄тра̄рвуда̄на̄м̇<span class="page-num"> — 260</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">О</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-ore-mana-bhalo-nahi-lage-e-somsara">оре мана бха̄ло на̄хи ла̄ге э сом̇са̄ра</a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-vaishnava-thakura-dayara-sagara">(охе) ваиш̣н̣ава т̣ха̄кура, дойа̄ра са̄гара<span class="page-num"> — 76</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-ohe-harinam-tava-mahima-apara">охе харина̄м, тава махима̄ апа̄ра<span class="page-num"> — 144</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">П</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-parama-karuna-pahu-dui-jana">парама корун̣а, паху̐ дӯи джана<span class="page-num"> — 115</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-pita-varana-kali-pavana-gora">пита-баран̣а кали-па̄вана гора̄<span class="page-num"> — 184</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-prabhu-kohe-kohilan-ei-mahamantra">«прабху кохе, — кохила̄н̇ эи маха̄мантра<span class="page-num"> — 249</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-prabhu-tava-pada-juge-mora-nivedana">прабху тава пада-джуге мора ниведана<span class="page-num"> — 186</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-emona-durmati-samsara-bhitore">(прабху хе!) эмона дурмати, сам̇са̄ра бхиторе<span class="page-num"> — 154</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-pralaya-payodhi-jale-dhritavan-asi-vedam">пралайа-пайодхи-джале дхр̣тава̄н аси ведам̇<span class="page-num"> — 225</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-dhvasta-sarvvabhauma-vada-navya-tarka-shiankaram">према-дха̄ма-дивйа-дӣргха-деха-дева-нандитам̇<span class="page-num"> — 88</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-prema-mugdha-nritya-kirtanakularitantikam">према-мугдха-нр̣тйа-кӣрттана̄кула̄рита̄нтикам̇<span class="page-num"> — 96</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-prema-mugdha-rudra-raja-shiaurya-virya-vikramam">према-мугдха-рудра-ра̄джа-ш́аурййа-вирййа-викрамам̇<span class="page-num"> — 92</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-prerakam-prachya-pashchatya-shishyanam">преракам̇ пра̄чйа-па̄ш́ча̄тйа ш́иш̣йа̄н̣а̄м̇<span class="page-num"> — 277</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-pujya-sri-guru-varga-vandita">пӯджйа-ш́рӣ-гуру-варга-вандита<span class="page-num"> — 12</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">Р</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-radha-krishna-bol-bol-bolo-re-sobai">‘рāдхā-кр̣ш̣н̣а’ бол бол боло ре собāи<span class="page-num"> — 250</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-radha-bhajane-jodi-moti-nahi-bhela">ра̄дха-бхаджане джоди моти на̄хи бхела̄<span class="page-num"> — 198</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-radha-krishna-prana-mora-jugala-kishora">ра̄дха̄-кр̣ш̣н̣а-пра̄н̣а мора джугала-киш́ора<span class="page-num"> — 205</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-radhakundatata-kunjakutira">ра̄дха̄-кун̣д̣атат̣а-кун̃джа-кут̣ӣра<span class="page-num"> — 199</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-radha-madhava-kunja-bihari">ра̄дха̄-ма̄дхава (джайа) кун̃джа-биха̄рӣ<span class="page-num"> — 203</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-radhe-jaya-jaya-madhava-dayite">ра̄дхе джайа джайа ма̄дхава-дайите<span class="page-num"> — 200</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-radhika-charana-padma-sakala-shreyera">ра̄дхика̄-чаран̣а-падма, сакала ш́рейера садма<span class="page-num"> — 196</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">С</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-sakala-vaishnava-pade-mora-namaskara">сакала ваиш̣н̣ава-паде мора намаска̄ра<span class="page-num"> — 21</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-sakhi-go-kemote-dhoribo-paran">сакхи го кемоте дхорибо пара̄н̣<span class="page-num"> — 191</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-samsara-davanala-lidha-loka">сам̇са̄ра-да̄ва̄нала-лӣд̣ха-лока<span class="page-num"> — 48</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-samudra-sambhava-gabhi-surabhi-apana">самудра-самбхава̄ га̄бхӣ сурабхӣ а̄пана<span class="page-num"> — 277</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-sarach-chandra-bhrantim-sphurad-amala-kantim">сарач-чандра-бхра̄нтим̇ спхурад-амала-ка̄нтим̇</a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-sarvasva-tomara-charane-sanpiya">сарвасва тома̄ра, чаран̣е са̐пийа̄<span class="page-num"> — 163</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-sujanarvuda-radhita-pada-yugam">суджана̄рвуда-ра̄дхита-па̄да-йугам̇<span class="page-num"> — 78</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-sutra-vritti-tippanishta-sukshma-vachanadbhutam">сӯтра-вр̣тти-т̣иппанӣш̣т̣а-сӯкш̣ма-ва̄чана̄дбхутам̇<span class="page-num"> — 85</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">Т</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-tatala-saikate-vari-bindu-sama">та̄тала саикате ба̄ри бинду-сама<span class="page-num"> — 164</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-tumi-sarveshvareshvara-vrajendra-kumara">туми сарвеш́вареш́вара, браджендра-кума̄ра!<span class="page-num"> — 162</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-tuhu-daya-sagara-tarayite-prani">туху̐ дойа̄-са̄гара та̄райите пра̄н̣ӣ<span class="page-num"> — 185</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-thakura-vaishnava-gana-kori-ei-nivedana">т̣ха̄кура ваиш̣н̣ава ган̣а кори эи ниведана<span class="page-num"> — 54</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-thakura-vaishnava-pada-avanira">т̣ха̄кура-ваиш̣н̣ава-пада, аванӣра сусампада<span class="page-num"> — 134</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">У</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-udilo-aruna-puraba-bhage">удило арун̣а пӯраба-бха̄ге<span class="page-num"> — 42</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">Х</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-hari-bole-modera-gaura-elo">‘хари’ боле’ модера гаура эло<span class="page-num"> — 251</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-hare-krishna-hare-krishna-krishna-krishna-hare-hare">харе кр̣ш̣н̣а харе кр̣ш̣н̣а кр̣ш̣н̣а кр̣ш̣н̣а харе харе<span class="page-num"> — 24</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-hari-nama-mahamantra-sarva-mantra-sara">хари-на̄ма маха̄мантра сарва-мантра-са̄ра<span class="page-num"> — 26</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-hari-haraye-namah-krishna-yadavaya-namah">хари харайе намах̣ кр̣ш̣н̣а джа̄дава̄йа намах̣<span class="page-num"> — 55</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-hari-hari-biphole-janama-goyainu">хари хари! бипхоле джанама гойа̄ину<span class="page-num"> — 159</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-hari-he-prapanche-podiya-agati-hoiya">хари хе! прапан̃че под̣ийа̄, агати хоийа̄<span class="page-num"> — 158</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-hari-he-dayala-mora-jaya-radha-natha">хари хе дайа̄ла мора джайа ра̄дха̄-на̄тха<span class="page-num"> — 165</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-ha-ha-bhaktivinoda-thakkura-guroh">ха̄ ха̄ бхактивинода-т̣ха̄кура! гурох̣!<span class="page-num"> — 254</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-he-deva-bhavantam-vande">хе дева бхавантам̇ ванде<span class="page-num"> — 202</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">Ч</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-chaudda-shiata-sata-shiake-masa-je-phalgun">чаудда-ш́ата са̄та-ш́аке ма̄са дже пха̄лгун<span class="page-num"> — 242</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-cheto-darpana-marjanam">чето-дарпан̣а-ма̄рджанам̇<span class="page-num"> — 182</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">Ш</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-guru-charana-padma-kevala-bhakati">ш́рӣ гуру чаран̣а-падма, кевала-бхакати-садма<span class="page-num"> — 74</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-krishna-kirtane-jodi-manasa-tohara">ш́рӣ-кр̣ш̣н̣а-кӣртане джоди ма̄наса тоха̄ра<span class="page-num"> — 185</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-jive-daya-kori">ш́рӣ-кр̣ш̣н̣а-чаитанйа прабху джӣве дойа̄ кори’<span class="page-num"> — 50</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-daya-kara-more">ш́рӣ кр̣ш̣н̣а чаитанйа прабху дойа̄ коро море<span class="page-num"> — 75</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-nityananda">ш́рӣ-кр̣ш̣н̣а-чаитанйа прабху нитйа̄нанда<span class="page-num"> — 24</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shriman-rasa-rasarambhi">ш́рӣман ра̄са-раса̄рамбхӣ<span class="page-num"> — 21</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shrimach-chaitanyapadau-chara">ш́рӣмач-чаитанйапа̄дау чара-камалайугау<span class="page-num"> — 264</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shrimach-chaitanya-sarasvata-mathavara-udgita-kirtir">ш́рӣмач-чаитанйа-са̄расвата-мат̣хавара-удгӣта-кӣртир</a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-rupa-manjari-pada-sei-mora-sampada">ш́рӣ-рӯпа-ман̃джарӣ-пада, сеи мора сампада<span class="page-num"> — 173</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-sanatananu-rupa-jiva-sampradayakam">ш́рӣ-сана̄тана̄ну-рӯпа-джӣва-сампрада̄йакам̇<span class="page-num"> — 101</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-svarupa-raya-rupa-jiva-bhava">ш́рӣ-сварӯпа-ра̄йа-рӯпа-джӣва-бха̄ва-самбхарам̇<span class="page-num"> — 274</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-siddhanta-sarasvatiti-vidito">ш́рӣ-сиддха̄нта-сарасватӣти видито<span class="page-num"> — 15</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shrita-kamala-kuchha-mandala-dhrita">ш́рита-камала̄ кучха-ман̣д̣ала дхр̣та-кун̣д̣ала хе<span class="page-num"> — 211</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shri-hari-vasare-hari-kirtana-vidhana">ш́рӣ хари-ва̄саре хари-кӣртана-видха̄на<span class="page-num"> — 238</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shiuddha-bhakata-charana-renu">ш́уддха-бхаката-чаран̣а-рен̣у<span class="page-num"> — 240</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-shiuno-he-rasika-jon-krishna-guna-aganon">ш́уно, хе расика джон кр̣ш̣н̣а-гун̣а аган̣он<span class="page-num"> — 209</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">Э</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-e-ghora-samsare-podiya-manava">э гхора сам̇са̄ре, под̣ийа̄ ма̄нава<span class="page-num"> — 148</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-ei-vara-karuna-kara-vaishnava-gosani">эи-ба̄ро коруна̄ коро ваиш̣н̣ава-госа̄и<span class="page-num"> — 136</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-emona-gauranga-bine-nahi-ara">эмона гаура̄н̇га бине на̄хи а̄ра<span class="page-num"> — 126</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-emona-durmati-samsara-bhitore">эмона дурмати, сам̇са̄ра бхиторе<span class="page-num"> — 154</span></a></p>
